attempt %d failed for claim %d of chain %d: %s\",\n                    attempt + 1,\n                    claim.claim_index,\n                    claim.chain_id,\n                    e,\n                )\n\n        # Exhausted retries — keep last result\n        claim.fol = claim.fol or \"\"\n        claim.z3_parseable = False\n        claim.fol_error = str(last_error) if last_error else \"unknown error\"\n        logger.warning(\n            \"Failed to formalize claim %d of chain %d after %d attempts: %s\",\n            claim.claim_index,\n            claim.chain_id,\n      \"\"\"Formalize a list of claims sequentially.\"\"\"\n        results = []\n        success = 0\n        for c in claims:\n            result = self.formalize(c, vocab)\n            results.append(result)\n            if result.z3_parseable:\n                success += 1\n        logger.info(\n            \"Batch formalization: %d/%d claims successfully formalized\",\n            success,\n            len(claims),\n        )\n        return results\n\n    def validate_z3_syntax(\n        self, fol_str: str, vocab: dict | None = None\n    ) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:\n        \"\"\"Validate a FOL string by evaluating it in a Z3 context.\n\n        Returns (True, None) on success, (False, error_message) on failure.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not fol_str or not completion_tokens=resp.usage.completion_tokens or 0,\n                module=\"autoformalize\",\n            )\n        return resp.choices[0].message.content.strip()\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _clean_fol(text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Strip markdown fences and stray whitespace from LLM output.\"\"\"\n        text = re.sub(r\"```(?:python)?\\s*\", \"\", text).strip()\n        text = text.rstrip(\"`\").strip()\n        # If multi-line, take only the first non-empty line (the expression)\n        for line in text.splitlines():\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line and not line.startswith(\"#\"):\n                return line\n        return text\nPYEOF"
}

{
  "server": "autodl-5090",
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/svs_formal_verification/svs_pipeline/formalization/vocab_normalizer.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Vocabulary normalizer: extract shared predicate/constant vocabularies from problems.\n\nEnsures all chains for a problem use the same predicate and constant names,\nwhich is critical for cross-chain logical coherence (CCLC) — without shared\nvocabulary, Z3 treats different symbol names as independent and the conjunction\nbecomes trivially satisfiable.\n\nInput: Problem (premises + hypothesis)\nOutput: dict with \"predicates\" and \"constants\" mappings\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\nimport json\nimport re\n\nfrom openai import OpenAI\n\nfrom ..types import Problem\nfrom ..config import APIConfig\nfrom ..cost_tracker import get_tracker\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Regex patterns for ProofWriter's stylized English\n_ENTITY_PAT = re.compile(\n    r\"\\b(?:The\\s+)?([A-Z][a-z]+(?:\\s+[A-Z][a-z]+)?)\\b\"\n    r\"(?=\\s+(?:is|likes|chases|sees|visits|eats|needs|does))\"\n)\n_UNARY_IS_PAT = re.compile(\n    r\"\\b(?:The\\s+)?(\\w+)\\s+is\\s+(?:not\\s+)?(\\w+)\\b\"\n)\n_BINARY_VERB_PAT = re.compile(\n    r\"\\b(?:The\\s+)?(\\w+)\\s+(likes|chases|sees|visits|eats|needs|does\\s+not\\s+\\w+|does\\s+\\w+)\\s+(?:the\\s+)?(\\w+)\\b\",\n    re.IGNORECASE,\n)\n\n_LLM_VOCAB_PROMPT = \"\"\"\\\nYou are a logic formalization assistant. Given a set of natural language premises and a hypothesis, \\\nextract a shared vocabulary of predicates and constants for first-order logic formalization.\n\n**Rules:**\n- Predicates are properties (unary) or relations (binary). Use CamelCase names.\n- Constants are named entities. Use lowercase snake_case.\n- Every predicate and constant that appears in the premises or hypothesis must be included.\n- Do NOT invent predicates/constants not grounded in the text.\n\n**Output format (strict JSON, no extra text):**\n```json\n{\n  \"predicates\": {\n    \"PredicateName\": {\"arity\": 1, \"description\": \"short description\"},\n    ...\n  },\n  \"constants\": {\n    \"entity_name\": {\"sort\": \"entity\", \"description\": \"short description\"},\n    ...\n  }\n}\n```\n\nPremises:\n{premises}\n\nHypothesis:\n{hypothesis}\n\nExtract the vocabulary now. Output only the JSON object.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass VocabNormalizer:\n    def __init__(self, api_config: APIConfig | None = None):\n        self._api = api_config\n        if api_config:\n            self._client = OpenAI(\n                base_url=api_config.base_url,\n                api_key=[REDACTED],\n                timeout=api_config.timeout,\n            )\n\n    def extract_vocab(self, problem: Problem) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Auto-select extraction mode: regex for ProofWriter, LLM for others.\"\"\"\n        if problem.dataset == \"proofwriter\":\n            vocab = self.normalize_vocab_regex(problem)\n            if vocab[\"predicates\"] or vocab[\"constants\"]:\n                logger.info(\n                    \"Regex extraction for %s: %d predicates, %d constants\",\n                    problem.id,\n                    len(vocab[\"predicates\"]),\n                    len(vocab[\"constants\"]),\n                )\n                return vocab\n            logger.warning(\n                \"Regex extraction empty for %s, falling back to LLM\", problem.id\n            )\n        return self.normalize_vocab_llm(problem)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Regex-based extraction (ProofWriter)\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def normalize_vocab_regex(self, problem: Problem) -> dict:\n        predicates: dict[str, dict] = {}\n        constants: dict[str, dict] = {}\n\n        all_text = problem.premises + [problem.hypothesis]\n\n        for sent in all_text:\n            self._extract_unary(sent, predicates, constants)\n            self._extract_binary(sent, predicates, constants)\n\n        # second pass: pick up entity names that only appeared as objects\n        for sent in all_text:\n            for m in _ENTITY_PAT.finditer(sent):\n                name = m.group(1).lower().replace(\" \", \"_\")\n                if name not in constants and name not in {\n                    p.lower() for p in predicates\n                }:\n                    constants[name] = {\"sort\": \"entity\", \"description\": name}\n\n        return {\"predicates\": predicates, \"constants\": constants}\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _extract_unary(\n        sent: str, predicates: dict, constants: dict\n    ) -> None:\n        for m in _UNARY_IS_PAT.finditer(sent):\n            subj = m.group(1).lower().replace(\" \", \"_\")\n            prop = m.group(2).capitalize()\n            if prop.lower() in (\"not\", \"a\", \"an\", \"the\"):\n                continue\n            predicates.setdefault(\n                prop, {\"arity\": 1, \"description\": f\"is {prop.lower()}\"}\n            )\n            constants.setdefault(\n                subj, {\"sort\": \"entity\", \"description\": subj}\n            )\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _extract_binary(\n        sent: str, predicates: dict, constants: dict\n    ) -> None:\n        for m in _BINARY_VERB_PAT.finditer(sent):\n            subj = m.group(1).lower().replace(\" \", \"_\")\n            verb = m.group(2).strip().split()[0].capitalize()\n            obj = m.group(3).lower().replace(\" \", \"_\")\n            pred_name = verb\n            # \"does not chase\" -> \"Chase\" (negation handled at FOL level)\n            if verb.lower() == \"does\":\n                parts = m.group(2).strip().split()\n                if len(parts) >= 2:\n                    pred_name = parts[-1].capitalize()\n            predicates.setdefault(\n                pred_name, {\"arity\": 2, \"description\": f\"{pred_name.lower()}s\"}\n            )\n            constants.setdefault(\n                subj, {\"sort\": \"entity\", \"description\": subj}\n            )\n            constants.setdefault(\n                obj, {\"sort\": \"entity\", \"description\": obj}\n            )\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # LLM-based extraction (FOLIO and fallback)\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def normalize_vocab_llm(self, problem: Problem) -> dict:\n        if not self._api:\n            raise ValueError(\"LLM vocab extraction requires an APIConfig\")\n\n        premises_text = \"\\n\".join(\n            f\"{i+1}. {p}\" for i, p in enumerate(problem.premises)\n        )\n        prompt = _LLM_VOCAB_PROMPT.format(\n            premises=premises_text, hypothesis=problem.hypothesis\n        )\n\n        last_error = None\n        for attempt in range(self._api.max_retries):\n            try:\n                resp = self._client.chat.completions.create(\n                    model=self._api.formalization_model,\n                    messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": prompt}],\n                    temperature=0.0,\n                    max_tokens=1024,\n                )\n                if hasattr(resp, 'usage') and resp.usage:\n                    get_tracker().record(\n                        model=self._api.formalization_model,\n                        prompt_tokens=resp.usage.prompt_tokens or 0,\n                        completion_tokens=resp.usage.completion_tokens or 0,\n                        module=\"vocab_normalizer\",\n                    )\n                text = resp.choices[0].message.content.strip()\n                vocab = self._parse_vocab_json(text)\n                logger.info(\n                    \"LLM vocab extraction for %s (attempt %d): %d predicates, %d constants\",\n                    problem.id,\n                    attempt + 1,\n                    len(vocab[\"predicates\"]),\n                    len(vocab[\"constants\"]),\n                )\n                return vocab\n            except Exception as e:\n                last_error = e\n                logger.warning(\n                    \"LLM vocab extraction attempt %d failed for %s: %s\",\n                    attempt + 1,\n                    problem.id,\n                    e,\n                )\n\n        logger.error(\n            \"LLM vocab extraction failed for %s after %d attempts: %s\",\n            problem.id,\n            self._api.max_retries,\n            last_error,\n        )\n        return {\"predicates\": {}, \"constants\": {}}\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _parse_vocab_json(text: str) -> dict:\n        # Strip markdown code fences if present\n        text = re.sub(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*\", \"\", text).strip()\n        text = text.rstrip(\"`\").strip()\n\n        data = json.loads(text)\n        vocab: dict = {\"predicates\": {}, \"constants\": {}}\n\n        for name, info in data.get(\"predicates\", {}).items():\n            vocab[\"predicates\"][name] = {\n                \"arity\": int(info.get(\"arity\", 1)),\n                \"description\": str(info.get(\"description\", \"\")),\n            }\n        for name, info in data.get(\"constants\", {}).items():\n            vocab[\"constants\"][name] = {\n                \"sort\": str(info.get(\"sort\", \"entity\")),\n                \"description\": str(info.get(\"description\", name)),\n            }\n        return vocab\nPYEOF"

{
  "server": "autodl-5090",
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/svs_formal_verification/svs_pipeline/verification/z3_checker.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Z3-based internal consistency checker for single reasoning chains.\n\nBuilds Z3 contexts from vocabulary, parses FOL strings into Z3 expressions,\nchecks satisfiability of claim conjunctions, and computes greedy MCS when\ninconsistencies are found.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nimport z3\n\nfrom ..types import AtomicClaim\nfrom ..config import VerificationConfig\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass Z3Checker:\n\n    def __init__(self, config: VerificationConfig):\n        self.config = config\n\n    def build_z3_context(self, vocab: dict) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Create Z3 symbols from a vocabulary dict.\n\n        vocab format:\n            {\"predicates\": {\"Green\": {\"arity\": 1}, \"Likes\": {\"arity\": 2}},\n             \"constants\":  {\"cat\": {\"sort\": \"entity\"}, \"dog\": {\"sort\": \"entity\"}}}\n\n        Returns dict mapping name -> z3 object (Functions and Consts).\n        \"\"\"\n        ctx = {}\n\n        for name, info in vocab.get(\"predicates\", {}).items():\n            arity = info[\"arity\"]\n            sorts = [z3.IntSort()] * arity + [z3.BoolSort()]\n            ctx[name] = z3.Function(name, *sorts)\n\n        for name, _info in vocab.get(\"constants\", {}).items():\n            ctx[name] = z3.Const(name, z3.IntSort())\n\n        return ctx\n\n    def parse_fol_to_z3(self, fol_str: str, z3_ctx: dict) -> Optional[z3.ExprRef]:\n        \"\"\"Parse a FOL string into a Z3 expression using the given context.\n\n        The fol_str is expected to be a Z3 Python expression string like\n        \"ForAll([x], Implies(Green(x), Big(x)))\".\n        \"\"\"\n        namespace = {\n            \"ForAll\": z3.ForAll,\n            \"Exists\": z3.Exists,\n            \"Implies\": z3.Implies,\n            \"And\": z3.And,\n            \"Or\": z3.Or,\n            \"Not\": z3.Not,\n            \"Int\": z3.Int,\n            \"IntSort\": z3.IntSort,\n            \"BoolSort\": z3.BoolSort,\n            \"Bool\": z3.Bool,\n            \"Function\": z3.Function,\n            \"Const\": z3.Const,\n            \"x\": z3.Int(\"x\"),\n            \"y\": z3.Int(\"y\"),\n            \"z\": z3.Int(\"z\"),\n            \"w\": z3.Int(\"w\"),\n        }\n        namespace.update(z3_ctx)\n\n        try:\n            expr = eval(fol_str, {\"__builtins__\": {}}, namespace)\n            if not isinstance(expr, z3.ExprRef):\n                logger.warning(\"FOL eval did not produce z3.ExprRef: %s\", fol_str)\n                return None\n            return expr\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.warning(\"Failed to parse FOL '%s': %s\", fol_str, e)\n            return None\n\n    def check_internal_consistency(\n        self, claims: list[AtomicClaim], vocab: dict, total_claims: int = 0\n    ) -> tuple[bool, float, int]:\n        \"\"\"Check whether formalized claims in a chain are internally consistent.\n\n        Args:\n            total_claims: total number of claims (including non-formalized).\n                          When >0, used as denominator for IC score so that\n                          chains with low formalization rates are penalized.\n\n        Returns:\n            (is_sat, ic_score, mcs_size)\n            - is_sat: True if the full conjunction is satisfiable\n            - ic_score: coverage-adjusted consistency score\n            - mcs_size: number of claims in the maximal consistent subset\n        \"\"\"\n        z3_ctx = self.build_z3_context(vocab)\n        formalized = [c for c in claims if c.z3_parseable and c.fol]\n\n        if not formalized:\n            return (True, 0.0, 0)\n\n        exprs = []\n        for claim in formalized:\n            expr = self.parse_fol_to_z3(claim.fol, z3_ctx)\n            if expr is not None:\n                exprs.append(expr)\n\n        if not exprs:\n            return (True, 0.0, 0)\n\n        denom = total_claims if total_claims > 0 else len(formalized)\n\n        solver = z3.Solver()\n        solver.set(\"timeout\", self.config.z3_timeout_ms)\n        solver.add(*exprs)\n\n        result = solver.check()\n\n        if result == z3.sat:\n            logger.info(\"Chain internally consistent: %d claims all SAT\", len(exprs))\n            ic_score = len(formalized) / denom\n            return (True, ic_score, len(formalized))\n\n        if result == z3.unsat:\n            logger.info(\"Chain inconsistent, computing greedy MCS\")\n            mcs, mcs_size = self.greedy_mcs(formalized, vocab)\n            ic_score = mcs_size / denom\n            return (False, ic_score, mcs_size)\n\n        # z3.unknown (timeout) — treat as consistent with penalty\n        logger.warning(\"Z3 returned unknown (timeout), treating as consistent\")\n        ic_score = len(formalized) / denom\n        return (True, ic_score, len(formalized))\n\n    def _check_sat_set(\n        self, claims: list[AtomicClaim], z3_ctx: dict\n    ) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if a set of claims is satisfiable.\"\"\"\n        exprs = []\n        for c in claims:\n            if c.z3_parseable and c.fol:\n                expr = self.parse_fol_to_z3(c.fol, z3_ctx)\n                if expr is not None:\n                    exprs.append(expr)\n        if not exprs:\n            return True\n        solver = z3.Solver()\n        solver.set(\"timeout\", self.config.z3_timeout_ms)\n        solver.add(*exprs)\n        return solver.check() == z3.sat\n\n    def greedy_mcs(\n        self, claims: list[AtomicClaim], vocab: dict\n    ) -> tuple[list[AtomicClaim], int]:\n        \"\"\"Compute a greedy maximal consistent subset (VERGE-style).\n\n        Iteratively tries removing each claim. If the removal makes the\n        remaining set SAT, keep the removal and stop (single-conflict case).\n        If no single removal suffices (multiple independent conflicts),\n        force-remove the last claim and repeat.\n        \"\"\"\n        z3_ctx = self.build_z3_context(vocab)\n        remaining = list(claims)\n\n        while len(remaining) > 1:\n            if self._check_sat_set(remaining, z3_ctx):\n                break\n\n            found_removal = False\n            for i in range(len(remaining) - 1, -1, -1):\n                candidate = remaining[i]\n                test_set = remaining[:i] + remaining[i + 1:]\n\n                if self._check_sat_set(test_set, z3_ctx):\n                    remaining = test_set\n                    found_removal = True\n                    logger.debug(\n                        \"Removed claim (chain %d, idx %d) — now SAT with %d claims\",\n                        candidate.chain_id, candidate.claim_index, len(remaining),\n                    )\n                    break\n\n            if not found_removal:\n                removed = remaining.pop()\n                logger.debug(\n                    \"Force-removed claim (chain %d, idx %d) — no single removal sufficed\",\n                    removed.chain_id, removed.claim_index,\n                )\n\n        return (remaining, len(remaining))\nPYEOF"

{
  "server": "autodl-5090",
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/svs_formal_verification/svs_pipeline/verification/cross_chain.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Cross-chain logical coherence (CCLC) checker.\n\nMerges formalized claims across chains sharing the same answer and checks\nwhether the combined set is satisfiable. Provides both full-group and\npairwise compatibility diagnostics.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\nimport re\nfrom itertools import combinations\n\nfrom ..types import AtomicClaim\nfrom ..config import VerificationConfig\nfrom .z3_checker import Z3Checker\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_PREDICATE_RE = re.compile(r'[A-Z][a-zA-Z]*(?=\\()')\n_CONSTANT_RE = re.compile(r'\\b[a-z][a-z_]*\\b')\n_QUANTIFIER_VARS = frozenset({\"x\", \"y\", \"z\", \"w\"})\n_Z3_KEYWORDS = frozenset({\n    \"and\", \"or\", \"not\", \"implies\", \"if\", \"else\", \"true\", \"false\",\n    \"forall\", \"exists\", \"int\", \"bool\",\n})\n\n\ndef _extract_predicates(fol: str) -> set[str]:\n    return set(_PREDICATE_RE.findall(fol))\n\n\ndef _extract_constants(fol: str) -> set[str]:\n    raw = set(_CONSTANT_RE.findall(fol))\n    return raw - _QUANTIFIER_VARS - _Z3_KEYWORDS\n\n\nclass CrossChainChecker:\n\n    def __init__(self, config: VerificationConfig):\n        self.config = config\n        self.z3_checker = Z3Checker(config)\n\n    def check_cross_chain_compatibility(\n        self, chain_claims: dict[int, list[AtomicClaim]], vocab: dict\n    ) -> tuple[bool, float, dict]:\n        \"\"\"Check cross-chain logical coherence for an answer group.\n\n        Args:\n            chain_claims: mapping chain_id -> list of AtomicClaim (same answer)\n            vocab: vocabulary dict for building Z3 context\n\n        Returns:\n            (is_sat, cclc_score, diagnostics)\n        \"\"\"\n        merged = []\n        per_chain_formalized: dict[int, list[AtomicClaim]] = {}\n        for chain_id, claims in chain_claims.items():\n            fc = [c for c in claims if c.z3_parseable and c.fol]\n            per_chain_formalized[chain_id] = fc\n            merged.extend(fc)\n\n        per_chain_predicates: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n        per_chain_constants: dict[int, set[str]] = {}\n        for chain_id, fc in per_chain_formalized.items():\n            preds: set[str] = set()\n            consts: set[str] = set()\n            for c in fc:\n                preds |= _extract_predicates(c.fol)\n                consts |= _extract_constants(c.fol)\n            per_chain_predicates[chain_id] = preds\n            per_chain_constants[chain_id] = consts\n\n        all_pred_sets = [s for s in per_chain_predicates.values() if s]\n        all_const_sets = [s for s in per_chain_constants.values() if s]\n\n        if len(all_pred_sets) >= 2:\n            pred_union = set().union(*all_pred_sets)\n            pred_intersect = all_pred_sets[0].copy()\n            for s in all_pred_sets[1:]:\n                pred_intersect &= s\n            predicate_overlap = len(pred_intersect) / len(pred_union) if pred_union else 0.0\n        else:\n            predicate_overlap = 1.0\n\n        if len(all_const_sets) >= 2:\n            const_union = set().union(*all_const_sets)\n            const_intersect = all_const_sets[0].copy()\n            for s in all_const_sets[1:]:\n                const_intersect &= s\n            constant_overlap = len(const_intersect) / len(const_union) if const_union else 0.0\n        else:\n            constant_overlap = 1.0\n\n        diagnostics: dict = {\n            \"total_merged_claims\": len(merged),\n            \"predicate_overlap\": predicate_overlap,\n            \"constant_overlap\": constant_overlap,\n            \"trivially_sat\": False,\n            \"per_chain_predicates\": {\n                cid: sorted(preds) for cid, preds in per_chain_predicates.items()\n            },\n        }\n\n        if not merged:\n            diagnostics[\"trivially_sat\"] = True\n            return (True, 1.0, diagnostics)\n\n        is_sat, ic_score, mcs_size = self.z3_checker.check_internal_consistency(\n            merged, vocab\n        )\n\n        if is_sat:\n            trivially_sat = predicate_overlap == 0.0 and len(all_pred_sets) >= 2\n            diagnostics[\"trivially_sat\"] = trivially_sat\n            if trivially_sat:\n                logger.warning(\n                    \"Cross-chain trivially SAT: %d merged claims have zero predicate \"\n                    \"overlap — vocab normalization may have failed\",\n                    len(merged),\n                )\n            else:\n                logger.info(\"Cross-chain SAT: %d merged claims consistent\", len(merged))\n            return (True, 1.0, diagnostics)\n\n        formalized_count = len(merged)\n        cclc_score = mcs_size / formalized_count if formalized_count > 0 else 1.0\n        logger.info(\n            \"Cross-chain UNSAT: MCS=%d/%d, cclc=%.3f\",\n            mcs_size, formalized_count, cclc_score,\n        )\n        return (False, cclc_score, diagnostics)\n\n    def pairwise_compatibility(\n        self, chain_claims: dict[int, list[AtomicClaim]], vocab: dict\n    ) -> dict[tuple[int, int], bool]:\n        \"\"\"Check pairwise compatibility between every pair of chains.\n\n        Returns:\n            dict mapping (chain_i, chain_j) -> is_compatible\n        \"\"\"\n        chain_ids = sorted(chain_claims.keys())\n        results = {}\n\n        for ci, cj in combinations(chain_ids, 2):\n            pair_claims = {ci: chain_claims[ci], cj: chain_claims[cj]}\n            is_sat, _, _ = self.check_cross_chain_compatibility(pair_claims, vocab)\n            results[(ci, cj)] = is_sat\n            logger.debug(\"Pair (%d, %d): %s\", ci, cj, \"SAT\" if is_sat else \"UNSAT\")\n\n        return results\nPYEOF"
}

{
  "server": "autodl-5090",
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/svs_formal_verification/svs_pipeline/verification/scoring.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Composite scoring and answer selection for SVS.\n\nCombines internal consistency (IC), formalization confidence (FC), and\ncross-chain logical coherence (CCLC) into a single group score per answer,\nthen selects the highest-scoring answer.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\nimport random\nfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\n\nfrom ..types import AtomicClaim, Chain, ChainVerification, GroupVerification, SVSResult\nfrom ..config import VerificationConfig\nfrom .z3_checker import Z3Checker\nfrom .cross_chain import CrossChainChecker\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass SVSScorer:\n\n    def __init__(self, config: VerificationConfig):\n        self.config = config\n\n    def score_chain(self, ic_score: float, fc_score: float) -> float:\n        return self.config.ic_weight * ic_score + self.config.fc_weight * fc_score\n\n    def score_group(self, chain_scores: list[float], cclc_score: float) -> float:\n        if not chain_scores:\n            return 0.0\n        mean_chain = sum(chain_scores) / len(chain_scores)\n        return mean_chain + self.config.cclc_lambda * cclc_score\n\n    def select_answer(\n        self,\n        chains: list[Chain],\n        chain_claims: dict[int, list[AtomicClaim]],\n        vocab: dict,\n        z3_checker: Z3Checker,\n        cross_checker: CrossChainChecker,\n    ) -> SVSResult:\n        \"\"\"Run full SVS scoring pipeline and select the best answer.\n\n        Args:\n            chains: all sampled chains for a problem\n            chain_claims: chain_id -> list of AtomicClaim\n            vocab: vocabulary dict for Z3 context\n            z3_checker: Z3Checker instance\n            cross_checker: CrossChainChecker instance\n\n        Returns:\n            SVSResult with selected_answer, group_scores, and SC baseline.\n            gold_label is left empty for the caller to fill.\n        \"\"\"\n        problem_id = chains[0].problem_id if chains else \"\"\n\n        answer_groups: dict[str, list[Chain]] = defaultdict(list)\n        for chain in chains:\n            answer_groups[chain.answer].append(chain)\n\n        group_scores: dict[str, float] = {}\n        group_verifications: dict[str, GroupVerification] = {}\n        all_chain_verifications: list[ChainVerification] = []\n\n        for answer, group_chains in answer_groups.items():\n            chain_verifications = []\n            chain_scores_list = []\n\n            for chain in group_chains:\n                claims = chain_claims.get(chain.chain_id, [])\n                total = len(claims)\n                formalized = [c for c in claims if c.z3_parseable and c.fol]\n                formalized_count = len(formalized)\n\n                fc_score = formalized_count / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n                ic_sat, ic_score, mcs_size = z3_checker.check_internal_consistency(\n                    claims, vocab, total_claims=total\n                )\n\n                cs = self.score_chain(ic_score, fc_score)\n                chain_scores_list.append(cs)\n\n                chain_verifications.append(\n                    ChainVerification(\n                        problem_id=problem_id,\n                        chain_id=chain.chain_id,\n                        answer=answer,\n                        total_claims=total,\n                        formalized_claims=formalized_count,\n                        ic_score=ic_score,\n                        fc_score=fc_score,\n                        ic_sat=ic_sat,\n                        mcs_size=mcs_size,\n                    )\n                )\n\n            group_chain_claims = {\n                chain.chain_id: chain_claims.get(chain.chain_id, [])\n                for chain in group_chains\n            }\n            cclc_sat, cclc_score, cclc_diagnostics = cross_checker.check_cross_chain_compatibility(\n                group_chain_claims, vocab\n            )\n\n            gs = self.score_group(chain_scores_list, cclc_score)\n            group_scores[answer] = gs\n\n            all_chain_verifications.extend(chain_verifications)\n\n            group_verifications[answer] = GroupVerification(\n                problem_id=problem_id,\n                answer=answer,\n                chain_verifications=chain_verifications,\n                cclc_score=cclc_score,\n                cclc_sat=cclc_sat,\n                group_score=gs,\n                cclc_diagnostics=cclc_diagnostics,\n            )\n\n            logger.info(\n                \"Answer '%s': %d chains, group_score=%.4f (cclc=%.3f, cclc_sat=%s)\",\n                answer, len(group_chains), gs, cclc_score, cclc_sat,\n            )\n\n        selected_answer = max(group_scores, key=group_scores.get)\n        sc_answer = self.majority_vote(chains)\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Problem %s: SVS='%s' (%.4f), SC='%s'\",\n            problem_id, selected_answer, group_scores[selected_answer], sc_answer,\n        )\n\n        return SVSResult(\n            problem_id=problem_id,\n            gold_label=\"\",\n            selected_answer=selected_answer,\n            sc_answer=sc_answer,\n            group_scores=group_scores,\n            correct_svs=False,\n            correct_sc=False,\n            chain_verifications=all_chain_verifications,\n        )\n\n    def majority_vote(self, chains: list[Chain]) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Self-consistency baseline: majority vote over chain answers.\"\"\"\n        if not chains:\n            return \"\"\n        counts = Counter(chain.answer for chain in chains)\n        max_count = counts.most_common(1)[0][1]\n        top_answers = [a for a, c in counts.items() if c == max_count]\n        return random.choice(top_answers)\nPYEOF"
}

r.correct_sc for r in results)\n    svs_only = sum(r.correct_svs and not r.correct_sc for r in results)\n    sc_only = sum(not r.correct_svs and r.correct_sc for r in results)\n    both_wrong = sum(not r.correct_svs and not r.correct_sc for r in results)\n    agreement = sum(r.selected_answer == r.sc_answer for r in results)\n\n    svs_acc = svs_correct / n\n    sc_acc = sc_correct / n\n\n    # per-label breakdown\n    per_label: dict[str, dict] = {}\n    by_label: dict[str, list[SVSResult]] = defaultdict(list)\n    for r in results:\n        by_label[r.gold_label].append(r)\n    for label in LABELS:\n        group = by_label[label]\n        gn = len(group)\n        if gn == 0:\n            per_label[label] = {\"n\": 0, \"svs_acc\": 0.0, \"sc_acc\": 0.0}\n        else:\n            per_label[label] = {\n                \"n\": gn,\n                \"svs_acc\": sum(r.correct_svs for r in group) / gn,\n                \"sc_acc\": sum(r.correct_sc for r in group) / gn,\n            }\n\n    # confusion matrix: predicted -> {gold: count}\n    confusion: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {\n        p: {g: 0 for g in LABELS} for p in LABELS\n    }\n    for r in results:\n        if r.selected_answer in confusion and r.gold_label in LABELS:\n            confusion[r.selected_answer][r.gold_label] += 1\n\n    return {\n        \"n\": n,\n        \"svs_accuracy\": svs_acc,\n        \"sc_accuracy\": sc_acc,\n        \"delta\": svs_acc - sc_acc,\n        \"svs_correct\": svs_correct,\n        \"sc_correct\": sc_correct,\n        \"both_correct\": both_correct,\n        \"svs_only_correct\": svs_only,\n        \"sc_only_correct\": sc_only,\n        \"both_wrong\": both_wrong,\n        \"agreement_rate\": agreement / n,\n        \"per_label\": per_label,\n        \"confusion\": confusion,\n    }\n\n\ndef format_report(metrics: dict) -> str:\n    if metrics[\"n\"] == 0:\n        return \"No results to report.\"\n\n    lines = [\n        f\"=== SVS Evaluation Report (n={metrics['n']}) ===\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"SVS accuracy:  {metrics['svs_accuracy']:.4f}  ({metrics['svs_correct']}/{metrics['n']})\",\n        f\"SC  accuracy:  {metrics['sc_accuracy']:.4f}  ({metrics['sc_correct']}/{metrics['n']})\",\n        f\"Delta (SVS-SC): {metrics['delta']:+.4f}\",\n        f\"Agreement rate:  {metrics['agreement_rate']:.4f}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"--- Overlap ---\",\n        f\"Both correct:      {metrics['both_correct']}\",\n        f\"SVS only correct:  {metrics['svs_only_correct']}\",\n        f\"SC  only correct:  {metrics['sc_only_correct']}\",\n        f\"Both wrong:        {metrics['both_wrong']}\",\n    ]\n\n    if metrics[\"per_label\"]:\n        lines += [\"\", \"--- Per Label ---\"]\n        for label, info in metrics[\"per_label\"].items():\n            if info[\"n\"] > 0:\n                lines.append(\n                    f\"  {label:>7s}  n={info['n']:<4d}  svs={info['svs_acc']:.4f}  sc={info['sc_acc']:.4f}\"\n                )\n\n    if metrics[\"confusion\"]:\n        lines += [\"\", \"--- Confusion (SVS predicted -> gold) ---\"]\n        lines.append(f\"  {'pred\\\\gold':>10s}  {'True':>6s}  {'False':>6s}  {'Unknown':>7s}\")\n        for pred in LABELS:\n            row = metrics[\"confusion\"].get(pred, {})\n            lines.append(\n                f\"  {pred:>10s}  {row.get('True',0):>6d}  {row.get('False',0):>6d}  {row.get('Unknown',0):>7d}\"\n            )\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\nPYEOF"
}
```
